ls: cannot access 'packages/observability/dist/error/': No such file or directory

'use client';

/**
 * apps/web/src/app/error.tsx
 *
 * Root error boundary for apps/web.
 * Integrates Sentry client capture and displays a luxury fallback recovery view.
 * Guarantees zero raw stack traces, database strings, or internal errors reach the client.
 */
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { captureClientError } from '@maihan/observability/client';
import { ErrorState } from '../components/design-system/ErrorState.js';

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  readonly error: Error & { digest?: string };
  readonly reset: () => void;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // Proactively capture to Sentry / observability
    try {
      captureClientError(error, {
        tags: { error_boundary: 'root_error_boundary', digest: error.digest ?? 'unknown' },
        extra: { message: error.message },
      });
    } catch {
      // Non-blocking guarantee
    }
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '4rem 1.5rem', maxWidth: '640px', margin: '0 auto', textAlign: 'center' }}>
      <ErrorState
        title="We encountered an unexpected disruption"
        recovery={
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', justifyContent: 'center', marginTop: '1.5rem' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={reset}
              className="button button--primary"
              style={{
                padding: '0.75rem 1.75rem',
                backgroundColor: '#0F172A',
                color: '#FFFFFF',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                border: 'none',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              Try Again
            </button>
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="button button--secondary"
              style={{
                padding: '0.75rem 1.75rem',
                backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC',
                color: '#0F172A',
                border: '1px solid #E2E8F0',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                textDecoration: 'none',
                display: 'inline-block',
              }}
            >
              Return to Storefront
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      >
        <p style={{ color: '#64748B', lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
          Our atelier systems have logged this event for investigation. Please retry or return to exploring gemstones.
        </p>
      </ErrorState>
    </div>
  );
}

```

  box-shadow: 0 10px 25px -5px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.3);
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  text-decoration: none;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.btn-primary:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  filter: brightness(1.05);
  box-shadow: 0 14px 28px -5px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  color: #08121e;
}
.btn-primary::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: -50%;
  left: -50%;
  width: 200%;
  height: 200%;
  background: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35), transparent);
  transform: rotate(45deg);
  animation: shimmer 4s infinite;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.btn-outline,
.btn-secondary {
  cursor: pointer;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.35);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-radius: 0.75rem;
  padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
  text-decoration: none;
  white-space: nowrap;
  box-shadow: 0 2px 8px -2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
}
.btn-outline:hover,
.btn-secondary:hover {
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.06);
  border-color: var(--color-gold);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  box-shadow: 0 6px 16px -4px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);
}

.btn-outline-dark {
  cursor: pointer;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: transparent;
  border: 1px solid rgba(13, 27, 42, 0.25);
  color: #0d1b2a;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-radius: 0.75rem;
  padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  text-decoration: none;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.btn-outline-dark:hover {
  background: rgba(13, 27, 42, 0.05);
  border-color: var(--color-gold-dark);
  color: var(--color-gold-dark);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

/* ── Luxury Cards ── */
.luxury-card {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  text-decoration: none;
}
.luxury-card::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 1.5px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.8), transparent);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.luxury-card--dark {
  background: linear-gradient(165deg, rgba(28, 46, 63, 0.5) 0%, rgba(19, 34, 52, 0.72) 42%, rgba(8, 18, 30, 0.92) 100%);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.18);
  color: #ffffff;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 36px -10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}
.luxury-card--dark:hover {
  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  transform: translateY(-4px);
  box-shadow: 0 18px 48px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

.luxury-card--light {
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.16);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03);
}
.luxury-card--light:hover {
  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  transform: translateY(-4px);
  box-shadow: 0 24px 48px -12px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.08);
}

/* ── Metric Cards ── */
.metric-card {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
  background: linear-gradient(165deg, rgba(28, 46, 63, 0.4) 0%, rgba(19, 34, 52, 0.65) 50%, rgba(8, 18, 30, 0.85) 100%);
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
}
.metric-card::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 1.5px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.8), transparent);
}
.metric-card__value {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 1.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin-bottom: 0.25rem;
  font-feature-settings: "lnum" 1, "tnum" 1;
}
.metric-card__label {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

/* ── Header Chrome & Silk & Stone 3-Zone Top Bar Navigation ── */
.site-header {
  background: rgba(250, 250, 248, 0.96);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.18);
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 50;
  backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
  width: 100%;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.site-header__container {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 0 1.5rem;
  height: 5rem;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1.5rem;
}

/* Zone 1: Brand Wordmark */
.site-header__brand {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  text-decoration: none;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  transition: opacity 0.2s ease;
}

.site-header__brand:hover {
  opacity: 0.92;
}

.site-header__wordmark {
  font-family: var(--font-serif);
  font-size: 1.625rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  color: #1a1a18;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: color 0.25s ease;
}

.site-header__brand:hover .site-header__wordmark {
  color: var(--color-gold);
}

.site-header__divider {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 1px;
  height: 0.875rem;
  background-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
}

.site-header__submark {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.625rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.25em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}

/* Zone 2: Navigation Links & MegaMenu */
.site-header__nav-zone {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex: 1;
  justify-content: center;
}

.mega-menu {
  position: relative;
}

.mega-menu__groups {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.75rem;
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
}

.mega-menu__group {
  position: relative;
}

.mega-menu__trigger {
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  color: #4a4740;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  padding: 0.625rem 0.5rem;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  transition: color 0.25s ease, transform 0.25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.mega-menu__trigger:hover,
.mega-menu__trigger[aria-expanded="true"] {
  color: #1a1a18;
}

.mega-menu__chevron {
  transition: transform 0.25s ease;
  opacity: 0.7;
}

.mega-menu__trigger[aria-expanded="true"] .mega-menu__chevron {
  transform: rotate(180deg);
  opacity: 1;
}

.mega-menu__panel {
  position: absolute;
  top: calc(100% + 0.75rem);
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.22);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
  min-width: 320px;
  max-width: 440px;
  max-height: 480px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  box-shadow: 0 30px 60px -15px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.12), 0 0 0 1px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
  z-index: 100;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(130px, 1fr));
  gap: 0.5rem 1rem;
}

.mega-menu__panel[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

.mega-menu__item {
  list-style: none;
}

.mega-menu__link {
  color: #4a4740;
  text-decoration: none;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  padding: 0.375rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 6px;
  display: block;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.mega-menu__link:hover {
  color: #1a1a18;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
  padding-left: 0.75rem;
}

/* Zone 3: Direct Utilities & Primary Action */
.site-header__actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.875rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.site-header__utility-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: #4a4740;
  text-decoration: none;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.15em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.site-header__utility-btn:hover {
  color: #1a1a18;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
}

.site-header__icon {
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.site-header__cta-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E8C96A 0%, #C9A84C 50%, #8B6914 100%);
  color: #FFFFFF !important;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  padding: 0.625rem 1.25rem;
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-decoration: none;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px -2px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.3);
  border: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.site-header__cta-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px -4px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  filter: brightness(1.06);
}

/* ── Footer Chrome ── */
.site-footer {
  background: #08121e;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  position: relative;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  padding: 4.5rem 2rem 2.5rem;
}
.site-footer::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 1.5px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.8), transparent);
}
.site-footer__container {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}
.site-footer__grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 2fr repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 3rem;
  margin-bottom: 3.5rem;
}
.site-footer__brand-title {
  font-family: var(--font-serif);
  font-size: 1.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--color-gold);
  margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.site-footer__tagline {
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);
  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;
  line-height: 1.6;
}
.site-footer__cities {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  color: var(--color-gold-pale);
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
}
.site-footer__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 9999px;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--color-gold-light);
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.site-footer__column-title {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;
}
.site-footer__links {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.625rem;
}
.site-footer__link {
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);
  text-decoration: none;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.site-footer__link:hover {
  color: var(--color-gold);
  padding-left: 0.25rem;
}
.site-footer__bottom {
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  padding-top: 2rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 1rem;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
}
.site-footer__disclosure {
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.45);
}
.site-footer__legal-bar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 1rem;
}
.site-footer__legal-links {
  display: flex;
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.site-footer__legal-link {
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: color 0.2s ease;
}
.site-footer__legal-link:hover {
  color: var(--color-gold);
}

/* ── Storefront Sections & Layouts ── */
.storefront-section {
  padding: 5rem 2rem;
  position: relative;
}
.storefront-section__container {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}
.storefront-section__header {
  text-align: center;
  max-width: 760px;
  margin: 0 auto 3.5rem auto;
}
.storefront-section__subtitle {
  font-size: 1.0625rem;
  line-height: 1.7;
  margin: 0;
}

/* ── Section Rhythm Backgrounds ── */
.bg-obsidian-deep {
  background-color: #08121e;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-primary-navy {
  background-color: #0d1b2a;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-midnight-obsidian {
  background-color: #080d19;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-crisp-white,
.bg-studio-white {
  background-color: #ffffff;
  color: var(--text-primary);
}
.bg-ivory-off-white,
.bg-warm-alabaster {
  background-color: #fafaf8;
  color: var(--text-primary);
}
.bg-gold-ticker {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #c9a84c 0%, #e8c96a 50%, #c9a84c 100%);
  color: #08121e;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.35);
}

/* ── Infinite Luxury Marquee Ticker ── */
@keyframes marqueeGlide {
  0% {
    transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translate3d(-50%, 0, 0);
  }
}

.marquee-container {
  overflow: hidden;
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #c9a84c 0%, #e8c96a 50%, #c9a84c 100%);
  color: #08121e;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  padding: 0.9rem 0;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.2);
}

.marquee-track {
  display: flex;
  width: max-content;
  will-change: transform;
  animation: marqueeGlide 35s linear infinite;
}

.marquee-track:hover {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

.marquee-content {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2.5rem;
  padding-right: 2.5rem;
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #08121e;
}

.marquee-dot {
  color: #08121e;
  opacity: 0.6;
}

/* ── Responsive Grids ── */
.grid-cards-3 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.grid-cards-4 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.grid-cards-6 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
  gap: 1rem;
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .site-footer__grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 2rem;
  }
  .grid-cards-4,
  .grid-cards-3 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
  .grid-cards-6 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .site-header {
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
  }
  .site-footer {
    padding: 3rem 1rem 1.5rem;
  }
  .site-footer__grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 2rem;
  }
  .storefront-section {
    padding: 3.5rem 1rem;
  }
  .grid-cards-4,
  .grid-cards-3,
  .grid-cards-6 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  .site-footer__legal-bar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
  }
}

/* ── Search Route & Filter Layout ── */
.search-page {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  min-height: 80vh;
}
.search-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 280px 1fr;
  gap: 2.5rem;
  align-items: start;
  margin-top: 1.5rem;
}
.filter-panel {
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.18);
  border-radius: 1rem;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 24px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
  position: sticky;
  top: 6rem;
}
.filter-group {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
  padding: 1rem 0;
}
.filter-group:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}
.filter-group__title {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem 0;
}
.filter-group__list {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  max-height: 220px;
  overflow-y: auto;
}
.filter-item-link {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  text-decoration: none;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 0.375rem;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.filter-item-link:hover {
  color: var(--text-primary);
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
}
.filter-item-link--selected {
  color: var(--gold-dark);
  font-weight: 600;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.12);
}
.filter-item__count {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}
.hero-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 0.85fr;
  gap: 3.5rem;
  align-items: center;
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .hero-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 2.5rem;
  }
}

.hero-showcase-card {
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 1.75rem;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 40px -10px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1), 0 0 0 1px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.06);
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
  text-align: left;
}

.hero-showcase-card:hover {
  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  box-shadow: 0 24px 60px -15px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.16);
  transform: translateY(-4px);
}

.hero-showcase-image {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 4/3;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08) 0%, #FAFAF8 70%);
  border-radius: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;
}

.hero-showcase-image img {
  max-width: 100%;
  max-height: 100%;
  object-fit: contain;
  transition: transform 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.hero-showcase-card:hover .hero-showcase-image img {
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.search-pills-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  margin-top: 1rem;
}

.search-pill-link {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);

}

- path: packages/config/src/index.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * packages/config — environment schema, feature flags, constants.
       *
       * Owns: env parsing, feature flags, constants.
       * Must never contain: the secrets themselves.
       *
       * This is the ONLY package permitted to read `process.env`. Per
       * technical_design/03 implementation constraint 3, no other package may read
       * it — the ESLint boundary config enforces that as a lint error.
       *
       * The env schema arrives now because a runtime dependency first needs
       * configuration: Phase 3 tasks 6-10 start the catalogue API process, which
       * needs its database, its optional Redis cache and its listener port.
       * Secrets themselves live in the platform secret manager only: never in the
       * repository, the database, a config table, a log or a trace (AGENTS.md 10).
       * Nothing parsed here is ever written to a log by this package.
       *
       * No schema library is introduced: the surface is four variables and each
       * validation is a plain predicate (a new runtime dependency would require an
       * ADR — SKILL_MATRIX_AND_TOOLING.md §13).
       */
      
      export interface RuntimeConfig {
        /** PostgreSQL connection string — the source of truth (ADR-002). */
        readonly databaseUrl: string;
        /** Redis URL — cache/rate-limit acceleration only; absent means bypass. */
        readonly redisUrl: string | null;
        /** Listener port for the catalogue API process. */
        readonly apiPort: number;
        /** Environment name; namespaces cache keys so environments never share. */
        readonly environment: string;
      }
      
      export class ConfigError extends Error {
        constructor(readonly variable: string, reason: string) {
          super(`[CONFIG] ${variable}: ${reason}`);
          this.name = 'ConfigError';
        }
      }
      
      const POSTGRES_SCHEMES = ['postgres://', 'postgresql://'];
      const REDIS_SCHEMES = ['redis://', 'rediss://'];
      
      function requireUrl(value: string | undefined, variable: string, schemes: string[]): string {
        if (value === undefined || value === '') {
          throw new ConfigError(variable, 'required but not set');
        }
        if (!schemes.some((scheme) => value.startsWith(scheme))) {
          throw new ConfigError(variable, `must start with ${schemes.join(' or ')}`);
        }
        return value;
      }
      
      function optionalUrl(value: string | undefined, variable: string, schemes: string[]): string | null {
        if (value === undefined || value === '') return null;
        return requireUrl(value, variable, schemes);
      }
      
      function parsePort(value: string | undefined): number {
        if (value === undefined || value === '') return 3000;
        const port = Number(value);
        if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
          throw new ConfigError('API_PORT', 'must be an integer between 1 and 65535');
        }
        return port;
      }
      
      /**
       * Parse and validate the runtime environment. Throws `ConfigError` on any
       * problem — configuration fails at startup, never mid-request.
       *
       *   DATABASE_URL  required  postgres://…        the source of truth
       *   REDIS_URL     optional  redis://…           absent => cache/rate-limits bypass
       *   API_PORT      optional  default 3000
       *   ENVIRONMENT   optional  default NODE_ENV or 'local' (cache namespace)
       */
      export function loadRuntimeConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): RuntimeConfig {
        return {
          databaseUrl: requireUrl(env['DATABASE_URL'], 'DATABASE_URL', POSTGRES_SCHEMES),
          redisUrl: optionalUrl(env['REDIS_URL'], 'REDIS_URL', REDIS_SCHEMES),
          apiPort: parsePort(env['API_PORT']),
          environment: env['ENVIRONMENT'] ?? env['NODE_ENV'] ?? 'local',
        };
      }
      
  referencedBy: {}

apps/web/src/lib/catalog-server.ts
import { createCatalogDatabase, createCatalogQueryPort, createPool } from '@maihan/db';
import {
  MemoryGenerationStore,
  NullCacheStore,
  cacheNamespace,
  createCatalogQueryService,
  type CatalogQueryService,
} from '../modules/catalog/index.js';

const globalStore = globalThis as unknown as {
  __maihanCatalogService?: CatalogQueryService;
  /** Test seam: when set, getService returns this instead of composing. */
  __maihanCatalogServiceOverride?: CatalogQueryService;
};

function getService(): CatalogQueryService {
  const override = globalStore.__maihanCatalogServiceOverride;
  if (override !== undefined) return override;

  const existing = globalStore.__maihanCatalogService;
  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;

  const config = loadRuntimeConfig();
  const pool = createPool(config.databaseUrl);
  const port = createCatalogQueryPort(createCatalogDatabase(pool));

  // Redis is OPTIONAL for the storefront (Task 7 degradation semantics):
  // without REDIS_URL the service runs its documented bypass — Postgres
  // remains truth on every read. Wiring the Redis store here is a
  // deployment-configuration step, not new architecture.
  const service = createCatalogQueryService({
    port,
    store: new NullCacheStore(),
    generations: new MemoryGenerationStore(),
    namespace: cacheNamespace(config.environment),
  });

  globalStore.__maihanCatalogService = service;
  return service;
}

/** Taxonomy-driven reads for Server Components (Task 3). */
export function catalogServer(): CatalogQueryService {
  return getService();
}

/**
 * Test-only override (imported exclusively by *.spec.ts): substitute a
 * service built over the FakeCatalogPort so page specs render without a
 * database. Production code never calls this.
 */

      # =============================================================================
      # Maihan Gems — local development database ONLY.
      # =============================================================================
      # Authority:
      #   INITIAL_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md section 36 task 2 — "Provision a disposable
      #     local PostgreSQL 16 ... for migrations".
      #   technical_design/02_TECH_STACK.md  — locked stack: PostgreSQL 16+, pgvector
      #     0.6+ with vector(1024) and HNSW.
      #   technical_design/04_DATABASE_IMPLEMENTATION.md — migration 0001 must be able
      #     to CREATE EXTENSION: vector, citext, pg_trgm, unaccent, btree_gin
      #     (pg_stat_statements optional).
      #   technical_design/29 section 3 — environments: `local` is NOT production.
      #
      # Image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16 was verified to contain PostgreSQL 16.15 with
      # `vector` 0.8.7 (architecture requires >= 0.6; Phase 0 validated 0.6.2) plus
      # citext, pg_trgm, unaccent, btree_gin and pg_stat_statements. The plain
      # `postgres:16-alpine` image was found to LACK `vector` and must never be used
      # here — doing so breaks the locked schema's HNSW indexes and vector(1024).
      #
      # This service is DEVELOPMENT ONLY. It is disposable. It holds no real data
      # ever: synthetic data everywhere, including staging (technical_design/32 rule 8).
      #
      # The password below is a local-only throwaway credential for a container that
      # binds to localhost only. It is NOT a repository secret: production secrets
      # live in the platform secret manager (AGENTS.md section 10) and never here.
      # =============================================================================
      
      services:
        db:
          image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16
          container_name: maihan-dev-db
          restart: unless-stopped
          environment:
            # Local-throwaway credential. Never reuse outside the container.
            POSTGRES_PASSWORD: maihan_local_dev_only
            POSTGRES_DB: maihan_dev
          ports:
            # Bind to localhost only — never expose to the network.
            - '127.0.0.1:5433:5432'
          volumes:
            - maihan_dev_dbdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data
          healthcheck:
            test: ['CMD-SHELL', 'pg_isready -U postgres -d maihan_dev']
            interval: 5s
            timeout: 3s
            retries: 10
      
      volumes:
        maihan_dev_dbdata:
      
  referencedBy: {}

> The Docker DB isn't running. Options: start compose DB (but is it migrated? The dev DB may be empty; local URL in .env example is port 5433). But .env has a real Neon dev DB URL. The Next dev server just isn't loading `.env` — wait, Next.js automatically loads `.env` from the app root (`apps/web/.env`), but the `.env` is at the repo root. Next.js loads env files from the project directory (apps/web). Since apps/web has no .env, DATABASE_URL is not set.
> 
> Best fix respecting repo rules: Next.js supports loading env from the project root... Actually Next only looks in the app dir. Options: 
> 1. Start local compose DB and set DATABASE_URL in apps/web? Creating a .env file with secrets — prohibited ("No secrets in the repository"). .env at root is presumably gitignored. Check .gitignore. If .env ignored, creating apps/web/.env that reads from root? Could symlink apps/web/.env -> ../../.env. Symlink is fine and shows no secret in git.
> 
> Actually simpler: Next dev can load env via the shell. The user presumably runs `pnpm dev` from apps/web themselves without env. How did this work before? Maybe previously root .env was picked up via next.config.ts? Let me check next.config.ts for dotenv handling.

```
/**
 * apps/web/next.config.ts
 *
 * Authority: technical_design/02 (Next.js App Router, rendering and HTTP
 * only), technical_design/08 (frontend architecture).
 *
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Bundler: webpack, not Turbopack (`next build --webpack`, see package.json).
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * This repository writes ESM source with explicit `.js` import specifiers
 * (the convention Node's ESM loader requires for the compiled `dist/` output
 * that `apps/web/src/api/**` runs under plain `node`). TypeScript resolves
 * `./x.js` to `./x.ts` under `moduleResolution: "Bundler"`, so `tsc -b` and
 * every package build are correct.
 *
 * Turbopack — the Next 16 default — does NOT resolve a `.js` specifier to a
 * `.ts`/`.tsx` file. `next build` therefore failed with 58 "Module not found:
 * Can't resolve '...js'" errors across the storefront tree. There is no
 * Turbopack option for extension aliasing: `turbopack.resolveExtensions`
 * only extends the list tried for *extensionless* specifiers, and
 * `turbopack.resolveAlias` maps module specifiers, not extensions. Tracked
 * upstream as vercel/next.js#82945 ("support importing .ts/.tsx via .js
 * extension (parity with webpack resolve.extensionAlias)").
 *
 * The supported fix is webpack's documented `resolve.extensionAlias`, which
 * Next exposes through this `webpack()` hook. It is applied to BOTH the
 * server and the client compilers, so client-boundary files (error.tsx etc.)
 * resolve too. No source file, tsconfig or type-safety setting is changed.
 *
 * Nothing else is tuned here: image domains, bundle policy and budgets land
 * with the Phase 4 tasks that own them.
 */
import type { NextConfig } from 'next';

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  webpack(config) {
    // `.js` -> `.ts`/`.tsx` (and `.mjs`/`.cjs` equivalents) so source written
    // for Node ESM output also resolves inside the Next bundler.
    config.resolve = config.resolve ?? {};
    config.resolve.extensionAlias = {
      ...(config.resolve.extensionAlias ?? {}),
      '.js': ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js'],
      '.mjs': ['.mts', '.mjs'],
      '.cjs': ['.cts', '.cjs'],
    };
    return config;
  },
};

export default nextConfig;
---GITIGNORE---

27:!.env.example
40:next-env.d.ts

> _Reasoning_
> The `.env` file exists at repo root with DATABASE_URL, but Next dev only loads `.env` from the app directory (apps/web). Earlier `ls apps/web/.env*` found nothing. So the fix: make Next load the root `.env`. Options:
> 1. Symlink apps/web/.env -> ../../.env
> 2. Run dev with cwd root — no, Next uses app dir.
> 
> Next.js loads env files from the project root (where next.config lives = apps/web). Next 16 also supports `experimental.env`? No. Simplest: create a symlink `apps/web/.env` → `../../.env`. Symlinks in git won't matter since .env is gitignored.
> 
> But wait — does the DB actually get used at runtime? The storefront page calls catalogServer() which creates a pool to the Neon DB. That's fine — dev branch DB, explicitly defined in .env by the user's setup. AGENTS.md prohibition is about AI writing to DB, not reading. We're not running migrations, just letting the app read from its configured dev DB. That's the app's normal behavior.
> 
> Alternatively, check whether compose local DB should be used — .env has the Neon URL set as active. Use it as-is.
> 
> Also there's an `.env.local`? No. Let me create symlink and restart dev server.

The root cause of the 500 is now clear: `DATABASE_URL` lives in the repo-root `.env`, but Next.js only loads env files from the app directory (`apps/web`), where none exists. I'll symlink it so Next picks it up:

}

/* Zone 2: Navigation Links & MegaMenu */
.site-header__nav-zone {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex: 1;
  justify-content: center;
}

.mega-menu {
  position: relative;
}

.mega-menu__groups {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.75rem;
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
}

.mega-menu__group {
  position: relative;
}

.mega-menu__trigger {
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  color: #4a4740;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  padding: 0.625rem 0.5rem;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  transition: color 0.25s ease, transform 0.25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.mega-menu__trigger:hover,
.mega-menu__trigger[aria-expanded="true"] {
  color: #1a1a18;
}

.mega-menu__chevron {
  transition: transform 0.25s ease;
  opacity: 0.7;
}

.mega-menu__trigger[aria-expanded="true"] .mega-menu__chevron {
  transform: rotate(180deg);
  opacity: 1;
}

.mega-menu__panel {
  position: absolute;
  top: calc(100% + 0.75rem);
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.22);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
  min-width: 320px;
  max-width: 440px;
  max-height: 480px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  box-shadow: 0 30px 60px -15px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.12), 0 0 0 1px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
  z-index: 100;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(130px, 1fr));
  gap: 0.5rem 1rem;
}

.mega-menu__panel[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

.mega-menu__item {
  list-style: none;
}

.mega-menu__link {
  color: #4a4740;
  text-decoration: none;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  padding: 0.375rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 6px;
  display: block;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.mega-menu__link:hover {
  color: #1a1a18;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
  padding-left: 0.75rem;
}

/* Zone 3: Direct Utilities & Primary Action */
.site-header__actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.875rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.site-header__utility-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: #4a4740;
  text-decoration: none;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.15em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.site-header__utility-btn:hover {
  color: #1a1a18;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
}

.site-header__icon {
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.site-header__cta-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E8C96A 0%, #C9A84C 50%, #8B6914 100%);
  color: #FFFFFF !important;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  padding: 0.625rem 1.25rem;
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-decoration: none;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px -2px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.3);
  border: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.site-header__cta-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px -4px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  filter: brightness(1.06);
}

/* ── Footer Chrome ── */
.site-footer {
  background: #08121e;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  position: relative;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  padding: 4.5rem 2rem 2.5rem;
}
.site-footer::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 1.5px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.8), transparent);
}
.site-footer__container {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}
.site-footer__grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 2fr repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 3rem;
  margin-bottom: 3.5rem;
}
.site-footer__brand-title {
  font-family: var(--font-serif);
  font-size: 1.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--color-gold);
  margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.site-footer__tagline {
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);
  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;
  line-height: 1.6;
}
.site-footer__cities {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  color: var(--color-gold-pale);
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
}
.site-footer__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 9999px;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--color-gold-light);
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.site-footer__column-title {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;
}
.site-footer__links {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.625rem;
}
.site-footer__link {
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);
  text-decoration: none;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.site-footer__link:hover {
  color: var(--color-gold);
  padding-left: 0.25rem;
}
.site-footer__bottom {
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  padding-top: 2rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 1rem;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
}
.site-footer__disclosure {
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.45);
}
.site-footer__legal-bar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 1rem;
}
.site-footer__legal-links {
  display: flex;
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.site-footer__legal-link {
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: color 0.2s ease;
}
.site-footer__legal-link:hover {
  color: var(--color-gold);
}

/* ── Storefront Sections & Layouts ── */
.storefront-section {
  padding: 5rem 2rem;
  position: relative;
}
.storefront-section__container {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}
.storefront-section__header {
  text-align: center;
  max-width: 760px;
  margin: 0 auto 3.5rem auto;
}
.storefront-section__subtitle {
  font-size: 1.0625rem;
  line-height: 1.7;
  margin: 0;
}

/* ── Section Rhythm Backgrounds ── */
.bg-obsidian-deep {
  background-color: #08121e;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-primary-navy {
  background-color: #0d1b2a;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-midnight-obsidian {
  background-color: #080d19;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.bg-crisp-white,
.bg-studio-white {
  background-color: #ffffff;
  color: var(--text-primary);
}
.bg-ivory-off-white,
.bg-warm-alabaster {
  background-color: #fafaf8;
  color: var(--text-primary);
}
.bg-gold-ticker {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #c9a84c 0%, #e8c96a 50%, #c9a84c 100%);
  color: #08121e;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.35);
}

/* ── Infinite Luxury Marquee Ticker ── */
@keyframes marqueeGlide {
  0% {
    transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translate3d(-50%, 0, 0);
  }
}

.marquee-container {
  overflow: hidden;
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #c9a84c 0%, #e8c96a 50%, #c9a84c 100%);
  color: #08121e;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  padding: 0.9rem 0;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.2);
}

.marquee-track {
  display: flex;
  width: max-content;
  will-change: transform;
  animation: marqueeGlide 35s linear infinite;
}

.marquee-track:hover {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

.marquee-content {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2.5rem;
  padding-right: 2.5rem;
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #08121e;
}

.marquee-dot {
  color: #08121e;
  opacity: 0.6;
}

/* ── Responsive Grids ── */
.grid-cards-3 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.grid-cards-4 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 1.5rem;
}
.grid-cards-6 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
  gap: 1rem;
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .site-footer__grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 2rem;
  }
  .grid-cards-4,
  .grid-cards-3 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
  .grid-cards-6 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .site-header {
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
  }
  .site-footer {
    padding: 3rem 1rem 1.5rem;
  }
  .site-footer__grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 2rem;
  }
  .storefront-section {
    padding: 3.5rem 1rem;
  }
  .grid-cards-4,
  .grid-cards-3,
  .grid-cards-6 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  .site-footer__legal-bar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
  }
}

/* ── Search Route & Filter Layout ── */
.search-page {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  min-height: 80vh;
}
.search-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 280px 1fr;
  gap: 2.5rem;
  align-items: start;
  margin-top: 1.5rem;
}
.filter-panel {
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.18);
  border-radius: 1rem;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 24px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
  position: sticky;
  top: 6rem;
}
.filter-group {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
  padding: 1rem 0;
}
.filter-group:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}
.filter-group__title {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem 0;
}
.filter-group__list {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  max-height: 220px;
  overflow-y: auto;
}
.filter-item-link {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  text-decoration: none;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 0.375rem;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.filter-item-link:hover {
  color: var(--text-primary);
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
}
.filter-item-link--selected {
  color: var(--gold-dark);
  font-weight: 600;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.12);
}
.filter-item__count {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}
.hero-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 0.85fr;
  gap: 3.5rem;
  align-items: center;
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .hero-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 2.5rem;
  }
}

.hero-showcase-card {
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 1.75rem;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 40px -10px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1), 0 0 0 1px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.06);
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
  text-align: left;
}

.hero-showcase-card:hover {
  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);
  box-shadow: 0 24px 60px -15px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.16);
  transform: translateY(-4px);
}

.hero-showcase-image {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 4/3;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08) 0%, #FAFAF8 70%);
  border-radius: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;
}

.hero-showcase-image img {
  max-width: 100%;
  max-height: 100%;
  object-fit: contain;
  transition: transform 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.hero-showcase-card:hover .hero-showcase-image img {
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.search-pills-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  margin-top: 1rem;
}

.search-pill-link {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.6875rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);
  border-radius: 9999px;
  padding: 0.3rem 0.75rem;
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.search-pill-link:hover {
  color: var(--gold-dark);
  border-color: var(--gold);
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.08);
}

.search-bar-form {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  max-width: 640px;
  margin: 1.5rem 0;
}
.search-input {
  flex: 1;
  background: #ffffff;
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  border-radius: 0.75rem;
  padding: 0.75rem 1.25rem;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.9375rem;
  outline: none;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px -2px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.06);
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.search-input:focus {
  border-color: var(--color-gold);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
}
.search-input::placeholder {
  color: var(--text-muted);
}
.active-chips-bar {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}
.active-chip {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.25);
  border-radius: 9999px;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.65rem;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  color: var(--gold-dark);
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}
.active-chip:hover {
  background: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.18);
  border-color: var(--color-gold);
}
.search-meta-bar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 1rem;
  padding:

0.875rem;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n}\n\n.dyo-summary-row--total {\n  border-bottom: none;\n  padding-top: 1.25rem;\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: 1.5rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n}\n\n.dyo-summary-row--total span:last-child {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 1.6rem;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n}\n\n/* ── Cart Styles ── */\n.cart-container {\n  max-width: 1000px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 2rem 1.5rem 5rem;\n}\n\n.cart-items-table {\n  width: 100%;\n  border-collapse: collapse;\n  margin: 2rem 0;\n}\n\n.cart-items-table th {\n  text-align: left;\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.2em;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  padding: 0.75rem 1rem;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);\n}\n\n.cart-items-table td {\n  padding: 1.25rem 1rem;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n  vertical-align: middle;\n}\n\n/* ── Silk \u0026 Stone Core Page Architecture ── */\n.page {\n  max-width: 1280px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 2.5rem 1.5rem 6rem;\n  min-height: 70vh;\n}\n\n.page h1 {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: clamp(2.25rem, 4vw, 3.25rem);\n  font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: -0.015em;\n  line-height: 1.15;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0.75rem 0 1rem 0;\n  text-wrap: balance;\n}\n\n.page-eyebrow {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.25em;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n  display: block;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;\n}\n\n.page-subtitle {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 1rem;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  max-width: 720px;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n\n.result-count {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.2em;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* ── Responsive Catalog Grids (Zero-Pill Discipline) ── */\n.product-grid,\n.settings-grid,\n.varieties-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));\n  gap: 2rem;\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0;\n  margin: 0 0 3rem 0;\n}\n\n.settings-card {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 1.75rem;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  min-height: 220px;\n  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.settings-card:hover {\n  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\n  box-shadow: 0 24px 48px -12px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1);\n}\n\n.settings-card__title {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: 1.375rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;\n  line-height: 1.25;\n}\n\n.settings-card__meta {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.8125rem;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n}\n\n.settings-card__action {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.18em;\n  color: var(--gold);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.35rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.settings-card__action:hover {\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n}\n\n/* ── Variety Hub \u0026 Mineral Cards ── */\n.variety-card {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 1.75rem;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.variety-card:hover {\n  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\n  box-shadow: 0 24px 48px -12px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1);\n}\n\n.variety-card__name {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: 1.5rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0 0 0.35rem 0;\n}\n\n.variety-card__species {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.18em;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.variety-card__links {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  padding-top: 1rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n}\n\n.variety-card__sublink {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.15em;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.variety-card__sublink:hover {\n  color: var(--gold);\n}\n\n/* ── Product Detail Layout (Two-Column Silk {\n    grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 1fr;\n    gap: 4rem;\n  }\n}\n\n.product-detail__summary {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 2.5rem;\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 24px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.product-detail__summary h1 {\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin-top: 0;\n}\n\n.product-detail__subtitle {\n - path: apps/web/src/proxy.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * apps/web/src/proxy.ts
       *
       * Legacy URL parity at the edge (Phase 4 Task 6).
       *
       * Authority: technical_design/34 section 7 ("incoming request -> normalise
       * path -> direct route match? serve -> else redirect lookup"), SoT 13.1.
       *
       * Runs BEFORE routing, so every one of the 44,218 observed source URLs either
       * resolves directly (the proxy returns `next()`) or 301/302s to a target
       * that resolves — or returns an honest 410. An unknown path is never redirected
       * to `/` or to an unrelated page: it falls through to the framework's 404 with
       * its search/category suggestions (doc 34 section 11).
       *
       * The resolver (and therefore this proxy) is Edge-safe: `redirects.ts` is
       * a pure module with no Node dependency and no database access. The mapping is
       * held in memory as the section 3 family rules plus the generated rows.
       */
      import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';
      
      import { createRedirectResolver } from './lib/redirects.js';
      import { GENERATED_REDIRECT_ROWS } from './lib/redirects-rows.generated.js';
      
      const resolver = createRedirectResolver(GENERATED_REDIRECT_ROWS);
      
      export function proxy(request: NextRequest): NextResponse {
        const { pathname, search } = request.nextUrl;
        const decision = resolver.resolve(pathname, search);
      
        if (decision === null) return NextResponse.next();
      
        if (decision.status === 410) {
          // A URL with no equivalent and no useful replacement: honest removal,
          // never a redirect to an unrelated page (doc 34 section 2).
          return new NextResponse('This page has been removed.', {
            status: 410,
            headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' },
          });
        }
      
        const url = request.nextUrl.clone();
        const [targetPath = '/', targetQuery] = decision.target.split('?');
        url.pathname = targetPath;
        url.search = targetQuery === undefined ? '' : `?${targetQuery}`;
        return NextResponse.redirect(url, decision.status);
      }
      
      /**
       * Skip Next internals, the API surface and static files. Everything else —
       * including paths that will legitimately serve a route or a 404 — goes through
       * the resolver, which returns `next()` unless the path is a real legacy URL.
       */
      export const config = {
        matcher: ['/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|api/).*)'],
      };
      
  referencedBy: {}

- path: apps/web/src/api/routes.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * apps/web/src/api/routes.ts
       *
       * Task 8 — the Phase 3 catalogue route table.
       *
       * Scope authority: plan Phase 3 outputs (`/api/v1/products`, `/products/{slug}`,
       * `/gemstones/{gemRef}`, `/settings`, `/settings/{sku}`, `/taxonomy/*`) and
       * SoT 11.2 / technical_design/06 "Catalog reads". The endpoint catalog
       * (paths, ids, request/response schemas) lives in `@maihan/contracts`
       * (`CATALOG_ENDPOINTS`) so OpenAPI and the router read ONE definition; this
       * module only adds the HTTP-runtime concern — which cache surface each
       * endpoint reads through. The parity spec proves every catalog entry has a
       * handler and vice versa, so a documented endpoint cannot lack an
       * implementation (and an undocumented one cannot exist).
       *
       * Every route is GET and PUBLIC (doc 06 route table): the visibility that
       * drives cache policy is read from the surface policy, never assumed.
       */
      import { CATALOG_ENDPOINTS } from '@maihan/contracts';
      import type { CatalogSurfaceName } from '../modules/catalog/index.js';
      
      export type RouteHandlerId = string;
      
      export interface RouteDefinition {
        readonly id: RouteHandlerId;
        readonly method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
        /** OpenAPI path template, `/api/v1` base included. */
        readonly path: string;
        /** Cache surface (Task 6 policy: scope, TTL, visibility). */
        readonly surface?: CatalogSurfaceName;
      }
      
      /** Endpoint id -> cache surface. Checked by the parity spec (no holes). */
      const SURFACE_BY_ENDPOINT: Record<string, CatalogSurfaceName> = {
        listProducts: 'productsList',
        productDetail: 'productDetail',
        gemstoneDetail: 'gemstoneDetail',
        listSettings: 'settingsList',
        settingVariant: 'settingVariant',
        settingDesign: 'settingDesign',
        taxonomyVarieties: 'taxonomy',
        taxonomyColours: 'taxonomy',
        taxonomyOrigins: 'taxonomy',
        taxonomyMetals: 'taxonomy',
        taxonomyFacets: 'taxonomy',
      };
      
      export const CATALOG_ROUTES: readonly RouteDefinition[] = CATALOG_ENDPOINTS.map((endpoint) => ({
        id: endpoint.id,
        method: 'GET',
        path: endpoint.path,
        surface: SURFACE_BY_ENDPOINT[endpoint.id] as CatalogSurfaceName,
      }));
      
      export const SEARCH_ROUTES: readonly RouteDefinition[] = [
        { id: 'search', method: 'POST', path: '/api/v1/search', surface: 'search' },
        { id: 'search', method: 'POST', path: '/search', surface: 'search' },
        { id: 'searchSuggest', method: 'GET', path: '/api/v1/search/suggest', surface: 'searchSuggest' },

      /**
       * apps/web/src/api/index.ts
       *
       * Tasks 8-10 — the catalogue API as a single framework-free handler:
       *
       *     (request: Request) => Promise<Response>
       *
       * Web-standard Request/Response on purpose: the Phase 4 Next.js route
       * handlers delegate to exactly this function (doc 08), the node:http adapter
       * in server.ts wraps it, and tests call it directly — one surface, no
       * framework coupling, no new runtime dependency.
       *
       * Pipeline (the order IS the architecture):
       *
       *   1. correlation ids           (doc 06: X-Trace-Id on every response)
       *   2. route lookup              (404 NOT_FOUND / 405 METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED)
       *   3. rate limit                (Task 9 gate: AFTER lookup — unknown paths
       *                                 are 404 without consuming budget — and
       *                                 BEFORE the handler, at the request
       *                                 boundary. Rejection = 429 RATE_LIMITED in
       *                                 the SoT 10.4 envelope with RateLimit-* and
       *                                 Retry-After; allowance attaches its
       *                                 RateLimit-* budget headers to whatever
       *                                 response follows.)
       *   4. handler                   (validate -> one service call -> serialize)
       *   5. cache headers             (Task 10: Cache-Control + Surrogate-Key
       *                                 derived from the handler's CacheInfo and
       *                                 the route's surface policy)
       *   6. error envelope            (SoT 10.4, one registry, no leaks)
       *
       * The handler returns `HandlerOutcome` (status/body/cache); only this module
       * turns it into a `Response`, so response policy can never fork per handler.
       */
      import { ContractError } from '@maihan/contracts';
      import { HANDLERS, type HandlerOutcome, type RouteContext } from './handlers.js';
      import { SEARCH_HANDLERS } from './search-handlers.js';
      import { COMMERCE_HANDLERS } from './commerce-handlers.js';
      import { ACCOUNT_HANDLERS } from './account-handlers.js';
      import { FAVORITES_HANDLERS } from './favorites-handlers.js';
      import { ADDON_HANDLERS } from './addon-handlers.js';
      import { DISCOUNT_HANDLERS } from './discount-handlers.js';
      import { REFUND_HANDLERS } from './refund-handlers.js';
      import {
        createDyoConfigurationHandler,
        getDyoConfigurationHandler,
        validateDyoConfigurationHandler,
        quoteDyoConfigurationHandler,
        getCompatibleSettingsHandler,
        addDyoToCartHandler,
      } from './dyo-handlers.js';
      import {
        createCertificateHandler,
        getCertificateHandler,
        processOcrRunHandler,
        reviewCertificateHandler,
        getPublicGemstoneCertificatesHandler,
      } from './certificate-handlers.js';
      import {
        aiChatHandler,
        aiHandoffHandler,
        aiGetConversationHandler,
      } from './ai-handlers.js';
      import {
        whatsappVerificationHandler,
        whatsappWebhookHandler,
        whatsappSendTemplateHandler,
      } from './whatsapp-handlers.js';
      import {
        getWebarAssetHandler,
        createArHandoffHandler,
        redeemArHandoffHandler,
      } from './webar-handlers.js';
      import {
        healthLiveHandler,
        healthReadyHandler,
        metricsHandler,
      } from './health-handlers.js';
      import {
        errorResponse,
        newRequestId,
        newTraceId,
        successResponse,
        type CorrelationIds,
      } from './envelope.js';
      import { lookupRoute } from './routes.js';
      import { applyCacheHeaders } from './headers.js';
      import type { RateLimitDecision, RateLimitGate } from './ratelimit/gate.js';
      import type { CatalogQueryService } from '../modules/catalog/index.js';
      import type { CatalogDatabase } from '@maihan/db';
      
      export type { RateLimitDecision, RateLimitGate } from './ratelimit/gate.js';
      
      export interface CatalogApiOptions {
        service: CatalogQueryService;
        db?: CatalogDatabase;
        /** Injected in tests for deterministic correlation ids. */
        ids?: Partial<CorrelationIds> | (() => CorrelationIds);
        /** Rate-limit gate (Task 9). Absent = no limiter wired (tests, local runs). */
        rateLimit?: RateLimitGate;
      }
      
      export type CatalogApi = (request: Request) => Promise<Response>;
      
      function resolveIds(options: CatalogApiOptions): CorrelationIds {
        const spec = options.ids;
        if (typeof spec === 'function') return spec();
        return {
          traceId: spec?.traceId ?? newTraceId(),
          requestId: spec?.requestId ?? newRequestId(),
        };
      }
      
      export function createCatalogApi(options: CatalogApiOptions): CatalogApi {
        return async function handle(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
          const ids = resolveIds(options);
          let url: URL;
          try {
            url = new URL(request.url);
          } catch {
            return errorResponse(new ContractError('VALIDATION_FAILED', 'Malformed request URL.'), ids);

```
# `apps/web/src/app/api`

**Reserved for Phase 4 Task 6+ / Phase 3 parity.**

The SoT 12.2 route tree pins an `api/` folder inside the Next.js app for route
handlers ("section 11" of the Source of Truth). The catalogue HTTP surface
already exists as a standalone `(Request)=>Response` handler in
`apps/web/src/api/` (NOT this folder), served by the node adapter in
`server.ts`.

This folder is intentionally **created-and-empty** in Task 1: Next.js's own
`app/api/**` for v1 route handlers will be SELF-CONTAINED here when a phase
needs it; mounting the existing handler into Next is a rendering-layer
decision, not a route-tree decision. UI route folders do NOT mount this
handler either: that is the scope of a later phase.

Do not place UI page files here. Do not implement handlers here before the
owning phase authorises it.

```

600;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n}\n\n.dyo-summary-row--total span:last-child {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 1.6rem;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n}\n\n/* ── Cart Styles ── */\n.cart-container {\n  max-width: 1000px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 2rem 1.5rem 5rem;\n}\n\n.cart-items-table {\n  width: 100%;\n  border-collapse: collapse;\n  margin: 2rem 0;\n}\n\n.cart-items-table th {\n  text-align: left;\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.2em;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  padding: 0.75rem 1rem;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.2);\n}\n\n.cart-items-table td {\n  padding: 1.25rem 1rem;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n  vertical-align: middle;\n}\n\n/* ── Silk \u0026 Stone Core Page Architecture ── */\n.page {\n  max-width: 1280px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 2.5rem 1.5rem 6rem;\n  min-height: 70vh;\n}\n\n.page h1 {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: clamp(2.25rem, 4vw, 3.25rem);\n  font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: -0.015em;\n  line-height: 1.15;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0.75rem 0 1rem 0;\n  text-wrap: balance;\n}\n\n.page-eyebrow {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.25em;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n  display: block;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;\n}\n\n.page-subtitle {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 1rem;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  max-width: 720px;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n\n.result-count {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.2em;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* ── Responsive Catalog Grids (Zero-Pill Discipline) ── */\n.product-grid,\n.settings-grid,\n.varieties-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));\n  gap: 2rem;\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0;\n  margin: 0 0 3rem 0;\n}\n\n.settings-card {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 1.75rem;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  min-height: 220px;\n  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.settings-card:hover {\n  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\n  box-shadow: 0 24px 48px -12px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1);\n}\n\n.settings-card__title {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: 1.375rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;\n  line-height: 1.25;\n}\n\n.settings-card__meta {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.8125rem;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  margin: 0 0 1.25rem 0;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n}\n\n.settings-card__action {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.18em;\n  color: var(--gold);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.35rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.settings-card__action:hover {\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n}\n\n/* ── Variety Hub \u0026 Mineral Cards ── */\n.variety-card {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 1.75rem;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.variety-card:hover {\n  border-color: rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.45);\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\n  box-shadow: 0 24px 48px -12px rgba(139, 105, 20, 0.1);\n}\n\n.variety-card__name {\n  font-family: var(--font-serif);\n  font-size: 1.5rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin: 0 0 0.35rem 0;\n}\n\n.variety-card__species {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.18em;\n  color: var(--gold-dark);\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.variety-card__links {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  padding-top: 1rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n}\n\n.variety-card__sublink {\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.15em;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.variety-card__sublink:hover {\n  color: var(--gold);\n}\n\n/* ── Product Detail Layout (Two-Column Silk {\n    grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 1fr;\n    gap: 4rem;\n  }\n}\n\n.product-detail__summary {\n  background: #ffffff;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(201, 168, 76, 0.15);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 2.5rem;\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 24px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);\n}\n\n.product-detail__summary h1 {\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  margin-top: 0;\n}\n\n.product-detail__subtitle {\n ## What was broken (two independent problems)

**1. `Module not found: Can't resolve '@maihan/observability/client'`**
The `packages/observability/dist/` build was stale — it predated the `./client` export and only contained `index.js`, not `dist/error/client.js`. Fixed by rebuilding:

```bash
pnpm -r --filter './packages/*' run build
```

**2. After that, `GET / → 500` with `ConfigError: [CONFIG] DATABASE_URL: required but not set`**
`DATABASE_URL` lives in the repo-root `.env`, but Next.js only loads `.env` from the app directory (`apps/web`). I created a symlink so Next picks it up:

```bash
ln -sfn ../../.env apps/web/.env
```

(Both `.env` and the symlink are gitignored, so nothing leaks.)

## Verification

- Killed the stale dev server (PID 4829) that was serving the old error, cleared `.next` cache, and restarted clean — `next dev` now reports `- Environments: .env`, the storefront compiles, and **`GET / → 200`** with the rendered page (confirmed via curl).
- **`GET /api/v1/health/live → 200`** (`{"status":"HEALTHY",...}`) — but note this endpoint is **not** served by the Next dev server by design: per `apps/web/src/app/api/README.md`, the catalogue HTTP API is a standalone handler served by the Node adapter (`apps/web/src/{api,src}/api/server.ts`), to be mounted into Next in a later phase. Running it standalone (`API_PORT=3002 node dist/api/server.js`) returns healthy with the database connected.
- `tsc -b` in `apps/web` passes (exit 0).

## How to run it

- Storefront: `cd apps/web && pnpm dev` → http://localhost:3000 (currently running as PID 10890)
- Catalogue API: `cd apps/web && node dist/api/server.js` (set `API_PORT` to avoid clashing with 3000; defaults to 3000)

The dev server is left running at http://localhost:3000 so you can browse the storefront now.

## Deliverables vs. request

The user asked: fix the errors and make sure the ecom app starts. Both done and verified through the actual interface (HTTP requests against the running server):

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| `GET /` | **200** — storefront renders |
| `GET /cart`, `GET /about` | **200 / 200** — additional routes render |
| `@maihan/observability/client` module resolution | Resolved (error.tsx compiles and `captureClientError` now executes in the error boundary) |
| `ConfigError: DATABASE_URL required but not set` | Resolved — `Reload env: .env` in the dev log, DB pool connects to the Neon dev DB |
| `tsc -b` in `apps/web` | Exit 0 |
| `GET /api/v1/health/live` | **200 healthy JSON** on the standalone API server (`API_PORT=3002 node dist/api/server.js`) |

## Notes / limitations

- The only remaining log entry after the fix is a **deprecation warning** from `pg-connection-string` about `sslmode=require` semantics in a future major version — not an error, no action taken (informational only).
- `/api/v1/health/live` still 404s inside the Next dev server by design: per `apps/web/src/app/api/README.md`, mounting the API handler into Next is explicitly deferred to a later phase. The endpoint is verified working on the standalone Node API server that owns it.
- Files changed: rebuild artifacts (`packages/*/dist`, gitignored) and one gitignored symlink `apps/web/.env -> ../../.env`. No source files were modified, so no other tests needed rerunning.
